## Output style

The reader has ADHD. Shape every response so it can be acted on:

1. Lead with the answer or next action: command, path, or snippet first.
2. Number multi-step work; one bounded action per step.
3. End with one next action doable in under two minutes.
4. Finish the current issue before raising a new one.
5. Restate progress each turn ("step 3 of 5 done").
6. Give time estimates in concrete units, never "a bit".
7. After a change, show what now works.
8. Errors: state location, cause, and fix. No drama.
9. Cap lists at 5 items.
10. No preamble, no recaps, no closers.

Exceptions: explain fully when asked to explain. Confirm before destructive actions. After three failed fixes, stop and name the doubtful assumption. If the request is ambiguous, ask one short question.
</INSTRUCTIONS>

## 需登录态的平台（按 doctor 的 active_backend 选命令）

```bash
# Twitter 搜索（twitter-cli 首选；失败重试链见 social.md）
twitter search "query" -n 10

# Reddit（无零配置路径：OpenCLI 或 rdt-cli，必须登录态）
opencli reddit search "query" -f yaml   # 桌面
rdt search "query" --limit 10            # 存量/服务器

# 小红书（桌面首选 OpenCLI）
opencli xiaohongshu search "query" -f yaml

# Facebook / Instagram（桌面 OpenCLI，复用浏览器登录态）
opencli facebook search "query" -f yaml
opencli facebook groups -f yaml
opencli instagram search "query" -f yaml       # 搜用户
opencli instagram user USERNAME -f yaml        # 读指定用户最近帖子
```

# 开发工具

GitHub CLI 

## GitHub (gh CLI)

GitHub 官方命令行工具，用于仓库、Issue、PR、Actions、Release 以及 API 访问。

```bash
# 认证
gh auth login
gh auth status

# 搜索
gh search repos "query" --sort stars --limit 10
gh search code "query" --language python

# 仓库
gh repo view owner/repo
gh repo clone owner/repo
gh repo create my-repo --private
gh repo fork owner/repo
gh repo fork owner/repo --clone
gh repo sync owner/repo

# Issues
gh issue list -R owner/repo --state open
gh issue view 123 -R owner/repo
gh issue create -R owner/repo --title "Title" --body "Body"

# Pull Requests
gh pr list -R owner/repo --state open
gh pr view 123 -R owner/repo
gh pr create -R owner/repo --title "Title" --body "Body"
gh pr checks 123 --repo owner/repo

# Actions / CI
gh run list --repo owner/repo --limit 10
gh run view <run-id> --repo owner/repo
gh run view <run-id> --repo owner/repo --log-failed
gh workflow list --repo owner/repo

# Releases
gh release list -R owner/repo
gh release create v1.0.0

# API
gh api /user
gh api repos/owner/repo

# JSON 输出
gh issue list --repo owner/repo --json number,title --jq '.[] | "\(.number): \(.title)"'
```

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要用<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2936" />
   过后没人知道那20条具体是什么。只在"这条 issue 之外没有任何值得记录的模式
   教训"时才允许省略,不能默认省略。
3. 在 issue 表下面另起一段写模式性教训——哪些问题反复出现(用第2步的
   `pattern_tag` 聚合,不用重新肉眼数一遍)、`origin` 分布(编码产生/修改引入/
   契约漂移/流程缺陷哪类最多,这个分布本身就是过程改进的信号)、`resolved_round`
   减 `first_seen_round` 算出的存活轮数最长的是哪条。这是对第2步的补充,不是替代
4. 如果项目有 `conversations/` 这类会话归档流程,顺手跑一遍,让内容复盘和过程
   复盘同步更新
5. **删除 `CURRENT-<type>.md` 前,确认它以及它所在目录不在 `.gitignore` 里**
   (`git check-ignore -v <path>`)。如果被忽略,说明这份文件从未进过 git 历史,
   "git log -p 能找回"这个安全网不成立,删除前必须先完整挪进 `RETROSPECTIVE.md`
   (含原始 Problem/Fix 细节,不能只留标题),不能假设"反正能从git找回"就放心
   压缩着删
6. **（仅检视人执行）** 确认第2步的完整 issue 表已经写进 `RETROSPECTIVE.md`,
   且已重新核对每条修复的实际效果无误后,再删除 `CURRENT-<type>.md`
   (`git rm`,如果可跟踪的话)

## 9. 与其他 review skill 的关系

- `code-review-graph` 装的 `review-changes` / `debug-issue` / `refactor-safely` /
  `explore-codebase`:这些是**图谱查询原语**,当作本 skill 第2、7步调用的基础
  设施使用,不是并列的独立审查流程
- 通用的一次性 `code-review-agent` 式 skill:适合真正的单次审计(没有后续修复-
  复核循环的场景);一旦审查进入"改了再审"的循环,改用本 skill,不要两边混用
  导致报告格式不一致
- CI 校验(`ci-verify` 之类):是第7条停止条件的一部分,不是独立步骤,闭环判定
  必须把它纳入,不能只看severity清零就宣布完成

    name: ruff
    needs: contract-sources
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7

  test:
    name: pytest (${{ matrix.python-version }})
    needs: contract-sources
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 3.11（requires-python）；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本的行为差异。
        # NFR-001 要求逐事件一致，跨版本差异必须在实现阶段就暴露，而不是实验阶段。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      # PYTHONHASHSEED=0 只是【让普通测试可复现】，**不是**检测误用内置 hash() 的手段。
      # 恰恰相反：固定 seed 会让一个误用 hash() 的实现在每次 CI 中稳定通过，
      # 把跨进程随机盐这个缺陷藏起来（第 36 章 P1-U03）。
      #
      # 真正的确定性门必须【跨两个不同 seed 的独立进程】比较同一运行的事件摘要，
      # 两者不等即失败。它需要哈希实现存在才能建立，由 0.1.1 T602 / 退出条件 E4 负责。
      #
      # 覆盖率门槛：语句+分支合并覆盖率 ≥90%（T001b：87% 提升至 90% 后实测 91%）。
      - env:
          PYTHONHASHSEED: "0"
        run: pytest --cov=src/market_game_sim/book --cov=src/market_game_sim/ledger --cov-branch --cov-fail-under=90

# Feature Specification: Belief Testing Laboratory

**规格编号**：v0.1-belief-testing-laboratory  
**状态**：见 frontmatter（唯一状态真源）。当前里程碑 0.1.3 在研，0.1.1—0.1.2 已
达成退出。根规格转 Stable 的条件是 **0.1.4** 退出（完整 v0.1 签收）。  
**创建日期**：2026-07-31　**更新日期**：2026-08-02  
**关联 PRD**：[`../../market-game-sim-prd.md`](../../market-game-sim-prd.md) v0.4.0  
**架构**：[`design.md`](design.md)　**里程碑**：[`0.1.1`](0.1.1-minimal-kernel/spec.md) · [`0.1.2`](0.1.2-leverage-and-first-experiment/spec.md) · [`0.1.3`](0.1.3-robustness/spec.md) · [`0.1.4`](0.1.4-replay-and-report/spec.md)  
**已生效决策**：[ADR-001](../../decisions/001-numeric-and-serialization-contract.md)
（数值与序列化口径）、
[ADR-002](../../decisions/002-same-timestamp-event-scheduling.md)（事件调度与因果链）<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="11" />信息由谁拥有**和入口链接，不复制状态、需求、合同或设计正文。从本文
出发最多两次点击可到达任一权威文档。

## 信息 → 唯一拥有者

| 信息 | 唯一拥有者 |
|---|---|
| 产品目标、范围、安全边界 | [`docs/market-game-sim-prd.md`](market-game-sim-prd.md) |
| 全局模块边界与技术不变量 | [`docs/market-game-sim-architecture.md`](market-game-sim-architecture.md) |
| 指标、研究方法与解释边界 | [`docs/research/`](research/methodology.md)（methodology、metrics-dictionary） |
| 跨 Feature 实现合同 | [`docs/contracts/`](contracts/matching.md) |
| 长期架构决策（ADR） | [`docs/decisions/`](decisions/000-template.md) |
| Feature/里程碑行为与状态 | 对应 `spec.md`（见 [`docs/features/README.md`](features/README.md)） |
| Feature/里程碑实现方案 | 对应 `design.md`（见 [`docs/features/README.md`](features/README.md)） |
| requirement owner 与 exit | [`docs/features/0.1/traceability.json`](features/0.1/traceability.json) |
| 开发纪律与质量门 | [`docs/SOP.md`](SOP.md) |
| 当前项目入口和强提醒 | [`CLAUDE.md`](../CLAUDE.md) |
| 实验协议与结果索引 | [`docs/experiments/`](experiments/experiment-template.md) |
| 性能基准与判定协议 | [`benchmarks/`](../benchmarks/README.md) |
| 检视与复盘报告 | [`docs/reviews/`](reviews/RETROSPECTIVE.md) |

## 文档职责边界

- README、CLAUDE、版本 README 只是**派生入口**，不得成为状态/需求/合同的第二份
  机器可读声明。
- `spec.md` frontmatter 是状态唯一真源；design/tasks 不得声明独立状态。
- architecture 不复制字段级合同；Feature design 不重新定义全局不变量。
- release / RETROSPECTIVE 不是当前产品或实现真相源。

## 验证入口

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要用<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3046" />`resolved_round`
   减 `first_seen_round` 算出的存活轮数最长的是哪条。这是对第2步的补充,不是替代
4. 如果项目有 `conversations/` 这类会话归档流程,顺手跑一遍,让内容复盘和过程
   复盘同步更新
5. **删除 `CURRENT-<type>.md` 前,确认它以及它所在目录不在 `.gitignore` 里**
   (`git check-ignore -v <path>`)。如果被忽略,说明这份文件从未进过 git 历史,
   "git log -p 能找回"这个安全网不成立,删除前必须先完整挪进 `RETROSPECTIVE.md`
   (含原始 Problem/Fix 细节,不能只留标题),不能假设"反正能从git找回"就放心
   压缩着删
6. **（仅检视人执行）** 确认第2步的完整 issue 表已经写进 `RETROSPECTIVE.md`,
   且已重新核对每条修复的实际效果无误后,再删除 `CURRENT-<type>.md`
   (`git rm`,如果可跟踪的话)

## 9. 与其他 review skill 的关系

- `code-review-graph` 装的 `review-changes` / `debug-issue` / `refactor-safely` /
  `explore-codebase`:这些是**图谱查询原语**,当作本 skill 第2、7步调用的基础
  设施使用,不是并列的独立审查流程
- 通用的一次性 `code-review-agent` 式 skill:适合真正的单次审计(没有后续修复-
  复核循环的场景);一旦审查进入"改了再审"的循环,改用本 skill,不要两边混用
  导致报告格式不一致
- CI 校验(`ci-verify` 之类):是第7条停止条件的一部分,不是独立步骤,闭环判定
  必须把它纳入,不能只看severity清零就宣布完成

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing â€” never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes â€” different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) â€” the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite â€”
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree â€” see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess â€” wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="472" />...................................... [ 41%]

name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the tas<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3064" />diff_validator.py:46:        changed = {"model_family_id": "f2", "factor_architecture": "signal", "maint_bp": 600}

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要用<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4772" />地因为固定用着旧版本 ruff 一直没发现。

要求：
- `dev` 分组里的每个工具都要写上界（`ruff>=0.16,<0.17` 这种范围，不是裸
  `ruff>=0.6`），升级到新的 minor/major 版本必须是一次显式的、本地验证过
  的改动，不能靠 CI 自己在某次无关提交时"顺便"升上去；
- 遇到"CI 红、本地绿"且报错内容和这次改动的文件对不上号时，第一步先比较
  本地工具版本和 `pyproject.toml` 里声明的版本范围是否一致（`ruff --version`
  vs 锁定范围），这往往比去猜"CI 环境哪里特殊"更快定位到根因；
- 需要升级某个开发工具的锁定版本时，先在本地装同一个版本跑一遍
  `pytest`/`ruff check .`/`ruff format --check .` 复现 CI 会看到的结果，
  确认没有新增的误报或需要一并调整的配置（比如这次需要额外加
  `extend-exclude = ["*.md"]`），再提交版本号改动。

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1671" />B站, V2EX, Reddit, Facebook, Instagram（多后端/登录态命令组）

# 开发工具

GitHub CLI 

## GitHub (gh CLI)

GitHub 官方命令行工具，用于仓库、Issue、PR、Actions、Release 以及 API 访问。

```bash
# 认证
gh auth login
gh auth status

# 搜索
gh search repos "query" --sort stars --limit 10
gh search code "query" --language python

# 仓库
gh repo view owner/repo
gh repo clone owner/repo
gh repo create my-repo --private
gh repo fork owner/repo
gh repo fork owner/repo --clone
gh repo sync owner/repo

# Issues
gh issue list -R owner/repo --state open
gh issue view 123 -R owner/repo
gh issue create -R owner/repo --title "Title" --body "Body"

# Pull Requests
gh pr list -R owner/repo --state open
gh pr view 123 -R owner/repo
gh pr create -R owner/repo --title "Title" --body "Body"
gh pr checks 123 --repo owner/repo

# Actions / CI
gh run list --repo owner/repo --limit 10
gh run view <run-id> --repo owner/repo
gh run view <run-id> --repo owner/repo --log-failed
gh workflow list --repo owner/repo

# Releases
gh release list -R owner/repo
gh release create v1.0.0

# API
gh api /user
gh api repos/owner/repo

# JSON 输出
gh issue list --repo owner/repo --json number,title --jq '.[] | "\(.number): \(.title)"'
```

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/fo<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="9039" />INVARIANT_DEFINITION_TOKENS):

name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing â€” never report a task done with CI unchecked or red

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes â€” different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) â€” the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite â€”
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree â€” see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess â€” wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3208" />............................................... [ 41%]

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

 # 全局不变量指纹：出现在里程碑 design.md 即视为「重新定义」而非「引用」。
 _GLOBAL_INVARIANT_MARKERS = ("C1", "C2")
 
-# C1/C2 定义式的特征符号（方程内容）——出现即视为「复制/重定义」，纯引用指针不算。
-_INVARIANT_DEFINITION_TOKENS = ("Σ", "≡", "position_units", "wallet_units", "entry_notional")
+# C1/C2 定义式的可判定语法：同一行内 marker 后出现方程运算符（=、≡、Σ）。
+# 不用固定 token 列表（会漏判通用方程），也不跨行 lookahead（会误吞下一行普通描述）。
+_INVARIANT_DEFINITION_OPERATORS = ("=", "≡", "Σ")
 
 
 def _contains_invariant_definition(text: str, marker: str) -> bool:
     """判断 `marker`（C1/C2）是否为定义式而非引用。
 
-    定义式：`C1: Σ position_units ≡ 0` 这类带守恒方程符号的内容；引用式：
-    `C1/C2 见 docs/contracts/` 或 `C1: 参见…` 只指向所有权、无方程内容。
+    可判定规则：`C1:`/`C2:` 之后**同一行**出现方程运算符（`=`/`≡`/`Σ`）即视为定义式；
+    引用（`C1: 见 docs/contracts/…`、`C1/C2 见…`）同一行无运算符，故放行。不跨行
+    采样，因此下一行的普通字段描述不会误归到该 marker。
     """
-    for m in re.finditer(rf"{marker}\s*:", text):
-        tail = text[m.end() : m.end() + 120]
-        if any(tok in tail for tok in _INVARIANT_DEFINITION_TOKENS):
+    for m in re.finditer(rf"{re.escape(marker)}\s*:", text):
+        line_end = text.find("\n", m.end())
+        if line_end < 0:
+            line_end = len(text)
+        tail = text[m.end() : line_end]
+        if any(op in tail for op in _INVARIANT_DEFINITION_OPERATORS):
             return True
     return False

name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing â€” never report a task done with CI unchecked or red

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes â€” different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) â€” the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite â€”
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree â€” see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess â€” wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh ru<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3039" />............................................... [ 41%]

  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing â€” never report a task done with CI unchecked or red

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes â€” different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) â€” the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite â€”
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree â€” see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess â€” wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh ru<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2539" />..................................... [ 41%]

name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1440" />认行为。

这类"CI 因为工具自己升级而红、不是因为代码改动"的情况已经不是第一次让人
诧异了。2026-08-09 的一次具体表现：ruff 0.15→0.16 之间把 markdown 文件里
的代码块格式化从"需要 `--preview` 才生效"改成默认开启，`docs/reviews/`、
`conversations/` 下大量复盘报告和对话存档里贴的代码片段（从来没变过、也不该
被当成维护中的源码去格式化）被判定为"需要重新排版"，把 CI 的 `ruff` job
搞红，而本地因为固定用着旧版本 ruff 一直没发现。

要求：
- `dev` 分组里的每个工具都要写上界（`ruff>=0.16,<0.17` 这种范围，不是裸
  `ruff>=0.6`），升级到新的 minor/major 版本必须是一次显式的、本地验证过
  的改动，不能靠 CI 自己在某次无关提交时"顺便"升上去；
- 遇到"CI 红、本地绿"且报错内容和这次改动的文件对不上号时，第一步先比较
  本地工具版本和 `pyproject.toml` 里声明的版本范围是否一致（`ruff --version`
  vs 锁定范围），这往往比去猜"CI 环境哪里特殊"更快定位到根因；
- 需要升级某个开发工具的锁定版本时，先在本地装同一个版本跑一遍
  `pytest`/`ruff check .`/`ruff format --check .` 复现 CI 会看到的结果，
  确认没有新增的误报或需要一并调整的配置（比如这次需要额外加
  `extend-exclude = ["*.md"]`），再提交版本号改动。
